### Applied Digital's $36 billion order book - and the two customers it won't name

### The return button isn't a receipt: building a "waiting for your crypto" page on your own site

![The return button isn't a receipt: building a "waiting for your crypto" page on your own site](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=800%2Cheight=%2Cfit=scale-down%2Cgravity=auto%2Cformat=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fl9pnelv3oypzw3mla9w2.png>)

### Bitcoin supply metric hits 11-year low as new ‘capitulation’ signal arrives
